# AUDIT-2026-11-14 — PEMS2024 Regional Patient Care Protocols vs published site

**Generated:** 2026-11-14 · **Generator:** `audit/protocol-reconciliation/work/merge_audit.py` (re-runnable) · **Method:** `audit/protocol-reconciliation/work/00_method_and_notes.md`

## Scope and method

Every protocol page under `docs/protocols/**/*.md` (59 pages found by `find docs/protocols -name '*.md'`) was audited row by row against the flattened PEMS2024 text (`pems_chunks/PEMS2024_full.txt`) in 11 batches (A1, A2, B1, B2, C1, C2, D1, D2, F1, F2, M1). This document is a mechanical consolidation of those batch outputs: quotes are copied verbatim from the batch JSON/MD, no protocol was edited, and no clinical judgement was added beyond the binding severity rulings R-1..R-6 below. PEMS level codes were decoded from flattened PDF text per the method note; findings marked `inferred` must be confirmed against `PEMS2024.pdf` before any edit.

## Totals by severity (AFTER rulings R-1..R-3)

| Severity | Findings |
|---|---:|
| CRITICAL | 215 |
| MODERATE | 402 |
| MINOR | 90 |
| **Total** | **707** |

Batch totals as delivered: A1 64, A2 40, B1 74, B2 64, C1 65, C2 42, D1 75, D2 101, F1 35, F2 55, M1 92 = 707. Before rulings the batches carried CRITICAL 201 / MODERATE 416 / MINOR 90; R-1..R-3 moved **14** findings from MODERATE to CRITICAL, giving CRITICAL 215 / MODERATE 402 / MINOR 90.

Flagged but not re-rated: 80 findings carry `level_decode: inferred` (level decoded from split level tokens in the flattened text) and need a human PDF check before the edit; 9 findings carry an `[OWNER]` decision marker (R-6).

## Severity rulings applied (parent decision, task t_d3600e06)

| Rule | Ruling | Findings changed |
|---|---|---:|
| R-1 | IV/IO fluid bolus for hypotension/shock: any NS/LR fluid-bolus row whose PEMS indication is hypotension/shock (anaphylaxis, sepsis, burns, heat, haemorrhage, trauma, electrical, lightning, crush, cardiac arrest/post-ROSC) and whose site level is narrower than PEMS → CRITICAL. IV/IO access-only rows keep their batch rating. | 6 |
| R-2 | 12-lead ECG at EMT: any site row for a PEMS EMT-level 12-lead ECG that is narrowed (A/I/P or blank) or missing → CRITICAL in every protocol. | 7 |
| R-3 | EMR-level life-saving steps: CPR / chest compressions (adult and paediatric), AED / defibrillation pad application and bleeding control for PEMS EMR rows that are narrowed or missing → CRITICAL. | 1 |
| R-4 | Everything else keeps the batch-assigned severity — nothing outside R-1..R-3 was re-rated. | 0 |
| R-5 | `level_decode: inferred` findings keep their batch severity — flagged, not downgraded. | 0 |
| R-6 | Owner-decision items keep their batch severity and get `[OWNER]` in FIXLIST.md. | 0 (flag only) |

Fluid-bolus rows whose PEMS indication is not hypotension/shock (SVT `A2-30`, hyperglycaemia `M1-HG-05`) and IV/IO access-only rows (`D2-TR-06`) were left at their batch severity, per R-1.

## docs/protocols/administrative/01_STEMI_Field_Triage_PROC.md

PEMS: p.35 · status **FIXABLE** · verified `true` · 4 findings.

Coverage note: Full PEMS p.35 read (lines 202-212). Decision tree decoded from the flattened Yes/Yes/No/No ordering: PCI within 30 min by ground? -> closest listed PCI hospital; else HEMS within 30 min? -> HEMS to closest listed PCI hospital with surgical capability; else closest hospital. Site receiving-center table: 11 facilities, all phone numbers match the PEMS lists except the Mary Washington second number (F1-21). Surgical column matches PEMS HEMS list (Maryview, Bon Secours Memorial, Henrico, Mary Washington, Riverside, Sentara Heart, VCU = surgical; CarePlex, Williamsburg, Mary Immaculate, Spotsylvania = not). Row 1 (ECG within 5 min of arrival), Row 5 (closest ED for compromised airway/collapse) and Row 6 (activate STEMI alert within 30 min) match. NOT COUNTED: 'Pre-Hospital STEMI Alert' COR wording and the Name/DOB pearl are sourced from the PEMS Chest Pain pearl (out-of-batch line 615), not p.35. Row 7 (serial ECGs every 10 min) and Pearl 3 (printed or electronic ECG copy) are not on p.35 and could not be verified in-batch; not counted. Level gating: the site's P-only Row 2 is not counted, because PEMS p.35 assigns no levels; the Chest Pain pearl (I and P may interpret STEMI) belongs to the cardiac batch.

|---|---|---:|---|---|---|---|---|

(and) notifying the closest PCI center within 30 minutes EMS transport time. | **Routing - PCI Capable Hospital**<br>Transport to the closest PCI capable facility if EMS ground transport time is < 30 minutes. | MODERATE | Change row 3 to '<= 30 minutes' and make row 4 '> 30 minutes' so the boundary is covered exactly once. |
| AUD-0239 | F1-20 | 4 | Action verb (evaluate vs activate) | Activate Helicopter EMS (HEMS) Transport to Closest Listed PCI Capable Hospital w/ Surgical Capabilities Early Notification/Activation | **Routing - HEMS Consideration**<br>If ground transport to a PCI facility is > 30 minutes, evaluate Helicopter EMS (HEMS) transport within 30 minutes. | MODERATE | Replace 'evaluate' with 'activate' and state the destination (closest listed PCI-capable hospital with surgical capability). |
| AUD-0635 | F1-18 | 2 | Criteria (machine 'ACUTE MI' alternative omitted) | Cardiac symptoms AND • 12-lead ECG criteria of 1 mm (or more) ST elevation in 2 (or more) contiguous leads OR • 12-lead ECG interpretation with an “ACUTE MI” statement | **ECG Interpretation**<br>Identify STEMI based on cardiac symptoms AND 1mm+ ST elevation in 2+ contiguous leads. | MINOR | Add the 'OR machine ACUTE MI statement' branch to row 2 (the site's Decision Scheme section already has it, so row 2 is merely incomplete). |

Routing decode from the flattened flowchart: RACE >= 5 and < 24 h or unknown with AIC suspicion -> Comprehensive Stroke Center, unless it adds >= 30 min -> nearest Primary Stroke Center; RACE 1-4 or > 24 h -> closest Stroke Center (Acute Stroke Ready, Primary or Comprehensive). ## docs/protocols/cardiac/04_Post_Resuscitation_Care_PROC.md

PEMS: p.59-60 (Cardiac Arrest: Post ROSC Care, PEMS Jan 2024) · status **FIXABLE** · verified `true` · 12 findings.

Coverage note: Coverage complete: PEMS p.59 (CRITERIA, 8 treatment steps, no MC row) and p.60 (PEARLS, 5 bullets incl. H's and T's) read in full with no truncation. All 8 site rows plus the site Pearls (5 items) compared. Doses verified against PEMS: NS 1 L adult in 250-500 mL boluses; 20 mL/kg peds, max 60 mL/kg; NE 5-20 mcg/min; epi infusion 2-10 mcg/min; peds epi 0.1-1 mcg/kg/min, max 10 mcg/min; SBP targets <1 yr >70 and >1 yr >90; push-dose epi 5-10 mcg q2-3 min. PEMS concentration arithmetic checked: 4 mg/250 mL = 16 mcg/mL; 1 mg/100 mL = 10 mcg/mL; 100 mcg/10 mL = 10 mcg/mL. Site row 2, 3, 4 and 5 and pearls 1-5 match PEMS text except as listed. Level decode: all levels are a bare level token immediately before row text (direct). The PDF could not be checked (NeedsOcrError on pages 47 and 151), so the three CRITICAL items (A2-07, A2-09, A2-11) need a human check of PEMS p.59 in the PDF before developer edits.

Missing sections: `Follow Universal Patient Care / Initial Patient Contact protocol (EMR), p.59`

## docs/protocols/cardiac/04_Supraventricular_Tachycardia_PROC.md

PEMS: p.135-136 (Supraventricular Tachycardia, PEMS Jan 2024) · status **FIXABLE** · verified `false` · 14 findings.

Coverage note: Partial coverage. PEMS p.135 (criteria, stable and unstable pathways) was read in full. PEMS p.136 PEARLS are truncated in the reader output after 'Dosing of electrical therapy described above serves as a guideline only. Confirm agency dosing for cardioversion and de...'; the rest is unreadable and the PDF cannot be opened. Site pearls 4 (SYNC before cardioversion) and 5 (Torsades) cannot be matched to visible PEMS text and are not counted. Dividers (site rows 3 and 11) not flagged, per the batch instruction. Site row 2 (Assess patient stability) has no PEMS table row; it paraphrases the criteria and is not counted. Doses verified: adenosine 6 mg adult and 0.1 mg/kg peds (max 6 mg) with 20 mL / 10-20 mL flush; NS 250-500 mL or 20 mL/kg (max 60 mL/kg); cardioversion 100 J then 200 J adult, 1 J/kg then 2 J/kg peds; midazolam 2.5 mg IV/IO or 5 mg IN/IM, peds 0.05 mg/kg IV/IO or 0.1 mg/kg IM, max 2.5 mg IV/IO or 5 mg IM. Inferred levels (A2-30, A2-32, A2-36, A2-38, A2-39) inherit from the parent row. Human PDF check needed for CRITICAL items A2-31, A2-32, A2-35, A2-37, A2-38 before developer edits.

## docs/protocols/cardiac/04b_Aortic_Dissection_AAA_PROC.md

PEMS: NONE located in PEMS2024 (no 'Aortic Dissection' protocol in PEMS2024_full.txt) · status **MANUAL** · verified `false` · 0 findings.

Coverage note: MANUAL - no PEMS 2024 source for this protocol; no findings invented. (1) pems_chunks/PEMS2024_full.txt has no 'Aortic Dissection' protocol title. Case-insensitive search for 'aortic\|aneurysm' returns one hit only: the PEMS Hypotension & Shock pearl ('Pipe problem: ... such as aortic dissection or pulmonary embolism', p.114). (2) pems_chunks/Aortic_Dissection.txt is a mis-chunk: its content is the PEMS Hypotension and Shock page p.114-115, not an aortic dissection protocol. (3) pems_chunks/protocol_index.json ('Aortic Dissection' -> 'aortic dissection') and mapping.json point at the same Hypotension pearl and mis-chunked file. (4) pems_chunks/ODEMSA_Aortic_Dissection.txt (and .pdf) is the legacy ODEMSA Protocol 2-5 'REVISED 06/2015', not PEMS 2024. The site's own source line cites this ODEMSA 2015 document. (5) The PEMS 2024 PDF cannot be read to confirm (NeedsOcrError: pages 47 and 151 need OCR). Decision needed from a human: confirm whether PEMS 2024 has an Aortic Dissection protocol; if it does, the PEMS text must be supplied; if not, the site protocol's provenance (ODEMSA 2015) must be re-sourced. Non-PEMS observations, not counted: site pearl 6 ('DO NOT administer Aspirin if aortic dissection or AAA is suspected') is broader than the ODEMSA 2015 row 4 wording ('DO NOT administer ASA if acute MI is present in conjunction with suspected AAA or aortic dissection'); site pearls 5, 7, 8, 9 and the site row 11 [A] MC requirement have no counterpart in the ODEMSA 2015 text. The Norepinephrine chart matches the ODEMSA 2015 chart (spot-checked 45-50 kg: 4.50-25.00 mcg/min; 126-130 kg: 12.60-65.00). Level decode: not applicable.

No discrepancies recorded — status **MANUAL**: this page was not compared against a full PEMS protocol (see coverage note), so it is NOT verified.

## docs/protocols/medical/07_Hypotension_Shock_Non_Trauma_PROC.md

PEMS: p.113-116 · status **FIXABLE** · verified `false` · 10 findings.

Coverage note: Re-verified from source (PEMS p.113-116). The partly-done decode in work/00 is confirmed: rows 1-3 EMR, row 4 EMT, row 5 A, row 6 I (inferred, see HYP-06), row 7 I, row 8 [I], row 9 P, row 10 MC bare. Site row 10 (bare MC at all levels) is CORRECT. Drip charts (norepinephrine 5-20 mcg/min and epinephrine 2-10 mcg/min, PEMS p.115-116) match the site values exactly, and the mixing concentrations 16 mcg/mL and 10 mcg/mL and the push-dose dilution are correct. Pearls visible in the extraction match the site (not-trauma scope, pump/tank/pipe, paediatric late sign, three-way stopcock, CHF smaller-boluses caution); the tail was not readable, so the PPV and vasopressor-access pearls are not verified word for word. One level inference: HYP-06. Severity: fluid, vasopressor and push-dose level rows rated CRITICAL. No PDF raster check.

Missing sections: `PEMS p.114 pearl block: final sentences after 'consider earl[y administration of vasopressors]' are truncated in extraction; not verified.`

## docs/protocols/medical/07_OB_Emergencies_PROC.md

PEMS: p.141-149 (Childbirth p.141-145; Eclampsia p.147-148; Pregnancy Related Emergencies p.149) · status **FIXABLE** · verified `false` · 37 findings.

Coverage note: PEMS OB = three sections: Childbirth/Labor/Delivery (p.141-145, incl. breech, cord prolapse, shoulder dystocia; p.146 blank), Eclampsia (p.147-148; p.148 blank), Pregnancy Related Emergencies (p.149; uterine rupture, abruptio). Located by title, not by a single 'OB' heading. The Childbirth PEMS page 141 pearl block and the Eclampsia pearl block are truncated in extraction, so those tails were not read; the placenta/post-partum row (OB-22) has a truncated final sentence. Shoulder dystocia level (OB-04) is inferred. Severity: fluid, magnesium, midazolam, breech manoeuvre, placenta and neonatal-resuscitation omissions rated CRITICAL; the MC row (row 10) level cells are correct. No PDF raster check performed.

Missing sections: `Childbirth: placenta delivery and post-delivery bleeding management (PEMS p.141, final EMR row truncated in extraction).`; `Childbirth: neonatal assessment and Neonatal Resuscitation referral; delayed cord clamping; nuchal cord clamp/cut.`; `Eclampsia: EMT transport-to-L&D row; PEMS pearls (Mg maximum 4 g, IM midazolam bridge, Mg cautions, early hospital notification).`; `Pregnancy-Related Emergencies: EMR ABC/O2/shock-referral rows; abruptio age-risk pearl.`

## docs/protocols/respiratory/03_Airway_Inhalation_Irritants_PROC.md

PEMS: p.153-154 (Airway / Inhalation Irritants); ETI procedure p.247-248 (cross-referenced by row 10) · status **FIXABLE** · verified `true` · 9 findings.

Coverage note: All PEMS steps on p.153-154 (criteria, protocol rows, pearls, concept of treatment, Poison Control note) and all 12 site rows were compared. Site rows 2, 3, 4, 11 match PEMS levels (decon EMT; airway/O2 EMT via Airway Management procedure). Row 9 (Advanced Airway Assessment) has no direct PEMS row; its P-only level is covered by C2-07. Site pearl 1 (decon before treatment), HPI exposure list (7 agents), Poison Control number 1-800-222-1222 and concept of treatment match PEMS. Site pearls 5-8 (phosgene delayed edema, H2S knockdown/SCBA, ammonia vs chlorine, chemical pneumonitis/ARDS) are not found in the PEMS text read (p.153-154) and are un-sourced; flagged for content review, not counted. Dosing verified: albuterol 3 mL 0.083%; methylprednisolone 125 mg adult / 2 mg/kg max 125 mg peds; sodium bicarbonate 2 mL + 2 mL NS once. Level decodes: rows 1, 5-7, 10, 12 direct; row 8 inferred. Human PDF check recommended for p.153 (row 8 level) before developer edits.

PEMS: p.239-240 (Airway Management: FBAO thrusts EMT; FBAO laryngoscopy I; pearls p.240); p.124 (RD Stridor subsection, epinephrine A); p.247-248 (ETI); p.55 (Sedation Post Advanced Airway) · status **MANUAL** · verified `false` · 8 findings.

Findings measured against Airway Management p.239-240 (FBAO thrusts, laryngoscopy, BVM), the RD Stridor subsection p.124, ETI p.247-248 and Sedation Post Advanced Airway p.55. Site rows 3-8 (conscious abdominal/chest thrusts and infant back blows/chest thrusts; unconscious positioning and BLS adjuncts) match PEMS EMT-level FBAO and airway text and are NOT counted. Site pearls 1-4 (conscious vs unconscious algorithm, infant head-down, chest thrusts for obese/pregnant, paramedic red-dot for <12 ETI) match PEMS. Pearls 5-8 (no blind sweep, complete vs partial obstruction, post-obstruction transport, expanding-hematoma early airway) have no PEMS text in this section and are un-sourced; not counted. PEMS 'Consider' qualifier is not mirrored on the site (not a discrepancy). Site rows 1-6 checked; PEMS rows 1-6 checked. ## docs/protocols/toxicology/06_Nerve_Agents_PROC.md

PEMS: p.159-160 · status **FIXABLE** · verified `true` · 15 findings.

Coverage note: Full PEMS p.159-160 read; no truncation on these pages. Atropine row: PEMS doses (2 mg adult, 1 mg <40 kg, 2 mg >=40 kg, q5min up to 3) match the site dose text; only the level and trigger are wrong. DuoDote contents (2.1 mg atropine, 600 mg pralidoxime) match. PEMS has no standalone pralidoxime row or dose; pralidoxime is only in the DuoDote pearl, and the site likewise has none, so no finding. A 'Nerve Agents (Pediatric) (Exposure)' entry appears in the CBRNE table of contents (PEMS p.151) but its page was not found in the flattened text; a human must locate it before this audit can claim pediatric nerve-agent coverage. PDF not openable (OCR needed).

## docs/protocols/trauma/09_Crush_Syndrome_PROC.md

PEMS: p.91-92 · status **FIXABLE** · verified `true` · 9 findings.

Coverage note: Coverage: PEMS p.91-92 read in full (CRITERIA, 9 PROTOCOL rows including the bracketed hyperkalemia rows, and PEARLS). Verified correct: NaCl doses (1 L; 250-500 mL boluses; 20 mL/kg); sodium bicarbonate 1 mEq/kg, max 50 mEq, 20 mL flush; calcium chloride 1 g adult, 20 mg/kg pediatric, max 1,000 mg; repeat once; albuterol 0.083%; widened QRS thresholds (Adult >120 ms, Pediatric >90 ms); ECG triggers; MC row (row 9) matches the bare MC. Bracket levels [A] and [I] come from flattened text (the bracket tokens sit across row boundaries), so every bracket-derived finding needs human eyeball confirmation in the PDF before the developer edits.
